{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{interfaceenum}{enum}} \mbox{\hyperlink{uapi_2linux_2ethtool_8h_a7ab36d2420db2e7a028c1b1660ab8c9f}{ethtool\+\_\+fec\+\_\+config\+\_\+bits}}}

enum ethtool\+\_\+fec\+\_\+config\+\_\+bits -\/ flags definition of ethtool\+\_\+fec\+\_\+configuration @\+E\+T\+H\+T\+O\+O\+L\+\_\+\+F\+E\+C\+\_\+\+N\+O\+N\+E\+\_\+\+B\+IT\+: F\+EC mode configuration is not supported. Should not be used together with other bits. G\+ET only. @\+E\+T\+H\+T\+O\+O\+L\+\_\+\+F\+E\+C\+\_\+\+A\+U\+T\+O\+\_\+\+B\+IT\+: Select default/best F\+EC mode automatically, usually based link mode and S\+FP parameters read from module\textquotesingle{}s E\+E\+P\+R\+OM. This bit does {\itshape not} mean autonegotiation. @\+E\+T\+H\+T\+O\+O\+L\+\_\+\+F\+E\+C\+\_\+\+O\+F\+F\+\_\+\+B\+IT\+: No F\+EC Mode @\+E\+T\+H\+T\+O\+O\+L\+\_\+\+F\+E\+C\+\_\+\+R\+S\+\_\+\+B\+IT\+: Reed-\/\+Solomon F\+EC Mode @\+E\+T\+H\+T\+O\+O\+L\+\_\+\+F\+E\+C\+\_\+\+B\+A\+S\+E\+R\+\_\+\+B\+IT\+: Base-\/\+R/\+Reed-\/\+Solomon F\+EC Mode @\+E\+T\+H\+T\+O\+O\+L\+\_\+\+F\+E\+C\+\_\+\+L\+L\+R\+S\+\_\+\+B\+IT\+: Low Latency Reed Solomon F\+EC Mode (25G/50G Ethernet Consortium) \begin{DoxyEnumFields}{Enumerator}

enum ethtool\+\_\+stringset -\/ string set ID @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+S\+S\+\_\+\+T\+E\+ST\+: Self-\/test result names, for use with E\+T\+H\+T\+O\+O\+L\+\_\+\+T\+E\+ST @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+S\+S\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+TS\+: Statistic names, for use with E\+T\+H\+T\+O\+O\+L\+\_\+\+G\+S\+T\+A\+TS @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+S\+S\+\_\+\+P\+R\+I\+V\+\_\+\+F\+L\+A\+GS\+: Driver private flag names, for use with E\+T\+H\+T\+O\+O\+L\+\_\+\+G\+P\+F\+L\+A\+GS and E\+T\+H\+T\+O\+O\+L\+\_\+\+S\+P\+F\+L\+A\+GS @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+S\+S\+\_\+\+N\+T\+U\+P\+L\+E\+\_\+\+F\+I\+L\+T\+E\+RS\+: Previously used with E\+T\+H\+T\+O\+O\+L\+\_\+\+G\+R\+X\+N\+T\+U\+P\+LE; now deprecated @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+S\+S\+\_\+\+F\+E\+A\+T\+U\+R\+ES\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classDevice}{Device}} feature names @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+S\+S\+\_\+\+R\+S\+S\+\_\+\+H\+A\+S\+H\+\_\+\+F\+U\+N\+CS\+: R\+SS hush function names @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+S\+S\+\_\+\+T\+U\+N\+A\+B\+L\+ES\+: tunable names @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+S\+S\+\_\+\+P\+H\+Y\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+TS\+: Statistic names, for use with E\+T\+H\+T\+O\+O\+L\+\_\+\+G\+P\+H\+Y\+S\+T\+A\+TS @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+S\+S\+\_\+\+P\+H\+Y\+\_\+\+T\+U\+N\+A\+B\+L\+ES\+: P\+HY tunable names @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+S\+S\+\_\+\+L\+I\+N\+K\+\_\+\+M\+O\+D\+ES\+: link mode names @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+S\+S\+\_\+\+M\+S\+G\+\_\+\+C\+L\+A\+S\+S\+ES\+: debug message class names @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+S\+S\+\_\+\+W\+O\+L\+\_\+\+M\+O\+D\+ES\+: wake-\/on-\/lan modes @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+S\+S\+\_\+\+S\+O\+F\+\_\+\+T\+I\+M\+E\+S\+T\+A\+M\+P\+I\+NG\+: S\+O\+F\+\_\+\+T\+I\+M\+E\+S\+T\+A\+M\+P\+I\+N\+G\+\_\+$\ast$ flags @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+S\+S\+\_\+\+T\+S\+\_\+\+T\+X\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+ES\+: timestamping Tx types @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+S\+S\+\_\+\+T\+S\+\_\+\+R\+X\+\_\+\+F\+I\+L\+T\+E\+RS\+: timestamping Rx filters @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+S\+S\+\_\+\+U\+D\+P\+\_\+\+T\+U\+N\+N\+E\+L\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+ES\+: U\+DP tunnel types @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+S\+S\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+T\+S\+\_\+\+S\+TD\+: standardized stats @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+S\+S\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+T\+S\+\_\+\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+P\+HY\+: names of I\+E\+EE 802.\+3 P\+HY statistics @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+S\+S\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+T\+S\+\_\+\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+M\+AC\+: names of I\+E\+EE 802.\+3 M\+AC statistics @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+S\+S\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+T\+S\+\_\+\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+C\+T\+RL\+: names of I\+E\+EE 802.\+3 M\+AC Control statistics @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+S\+S\+\_\+\+S\+T\+A\+T\+S\+\_\+\+R\+M\+ON\+: names of R\+M\+ON statistics

\index{ethtool.h@{ethtool.h}!ethtool\_test\_flags@{ethtool\_test\_flags}}
\index{ethtool\_test\_flags@{ethtool\_test\_flags}!ethtool.h@{ethtool.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ethtool\_test\_flags}{ethtool\_test\_flags}}

enum ethtool\+\_\+test\+\_\+flags -\/ flags definition of \mbox{\hyperlink{structethtool__test}{ethtool\+\_\+test}} @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+T\+E\+S\+T\+\_\+\+F\+L\+\_\+\+O\+F\+F\+L\+I\+NE\+: if set perform online and offline tests, otherwise only online tests. @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+T\+E\+S\+T\+\_\+\+F\+L\+\_\+\+F\+A\+I\+L\+ED\+: Driver set this flag if test fails. @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+T\+E\+S\+T\+\_\+\+F\+L\+\_\+\+E\+X\+T\+E\+R\+N\+A\+L\+\_\+\+LB\+: Application request to perform external loopback test. @\+E\+T\+H\+\_\+\+T\+E\+S\+T\+\_\+\+F\+L\+\_\+\+E\+X\+T\+E\+R\+N\+A\+L\+\_\+\+L\+B\+\_\+\+D\+O\+NE\+: Driver performed the external loopback test \begin{DoxyEnumFields}{Enumerator}
